==============================
FILE: src/components/CartDrawer.tsx
==============================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { CartItem, NavView } from '../types';
import { X, Plus, Minus, Trash2, ShoppingBag, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, Tag } from 'lucide-react';
import { ProductPackagingView } from './ProductPackagingView';

interface CartDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  items: CartItem[];
  onUpdateQuantity: (productId: string, delta: number) => void;
  onRemoveItem: (productId: string) => void;
  onOpenCheckout: () => void;
  setCurrentView: (view: NavView) => void;
}

export const CartDrawer: React.FC<CartDrawerProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  items,
  onUpdateQuantity,
  onRemoveItem,
  onOpenCheckout,
  setCurrentView
}) => {
  const [promoCode, setPromoCode] = useState('');
  const [appliedPromo, setAppliedPromo] = useState<{ code: string; discountPercent: number } | null>(
    null
  );
  const [promoError, setPromoError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const rawSubtotal = items.reduce((acc, item) => acc + item.product.price * item.quantity, 0);
  const discountAmount = appliedPromo ? Math.round((rawSubtotal * appliedPromo.discountPercent) / 100) : 0;
  const subtotal = rawSubtotal - discountAmount;
  const freeShippingThreshold = 999;
  const shipping = subtotal >= freeShippingThreshold || subtotal === 0 ? 0 : 99;
  const total = subtotal + shipping;
  const freeShippingProgress = Math.min(100, Math.round((rawSubtotal / freeShippingThreshold) * 100));
  const amountToFreeShipping = Math.max(0, freeShippingThreshold - rawSubtotal);

  const handleApplyPromo = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPromoError(null);
    const cleanCode = promoCode.trim().toUpperCase();

    if (cleanCode === 'AEGIS10') {
      setAppliedPromo({ code: 'AEGIS10', discountPercent: 10 });
      setPromoCode('');
    } else if (cleanCode === 'WELCOME15') {
      setAppliedPromo({ code: 'WELCOME15', discountPercent: 15 });
      setPromoCode('');
    } else if (!cleanCode) {
      setPromoError('Please enter a valid coupon code.');
    } else {
      setPromoError('Code is invalid or has expired.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden bg-[#20231F]/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex justify-end animate-in fade-in">
      <div className="bg-[#F8F5EF] border-l border-[#CFC8BC] w-full max-w-md h-full flex flex-col justify-between shadow-2xl relative">
        {/* Top Header */}
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-[#CFC8BC] flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4 text-[#4B5848]" />
            <h2 className="font-serif-editorial text-xl font-medium text-[#20231F]">
              Your Shopping Bag ({items.reduce((acc, i) => acc + i.quantity, 0)})
            </h2>
          </div>
          <button
            id="cart-drawer-close-btn"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-[#F2EEE7] text-[#20231F] border border-transparent hover:border-[#CFC8BC] transition-colors"
            aria-label="Close shopping bag"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Free Shipping Progress Indicator */}
        <div className="px-6 py-3 bg-[#F2EEE7] border-b border-[#CFC8BC] text-left space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] font-mono-spec text-[#20231F]">
            {amountToFreeShipping === 0 ? (
              <span className="text-[#4B5848] font-bold">✓ YOU HAVE QUALIFIED FOR FREE SHIPPING</span>
            ) : (
              <span>
                Add <strong className="text-[#20231F]">₹{amountToFreeShipping}</strong> more for Free Delivery
              </span>
            )}
            <span className="text-[#5C625B]">{freeShippingProgress}%</span>
          </div>
          <div className="w-full h-1.5 bg-[#E8E1D6] rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-[#4B5848] transition-all duration-300"
              style={{ width: `${freeShippingProgress}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Cart Items List */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-4 text-left">
          {items.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-20 text-center space-y-4">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-[#F2EEE7] border border-[#CFC8BC] flex items-center justify-center mx-auto text-[#5C625B]">
                <ShoppingBag className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <h3 className="font-serif-editorial text-lg text-[#20231F]">
                  Your bag is currently empty
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#5C625B] max-w-xs mx-auto">
                  Discover our evidence-informed skincare protocols designed for everyday defense.
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  onClose();
                  setCurrentView('shop');
                }}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-[#4B5848] text-[#F8F5EF] text-xs font-mono-spec uppercase tracking-wider rounded-[3px]"
              >
                Explore Formulations
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            items.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.product.id}
                className="p-4 bg-[#F2EEE7] border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[4px] flex gap-4 items-center justify-between"
              >
                <div className="w-16 h-16 shrink-0 rounded-[2px] overflow-hidden border border-[#CFC8BC]">
                  <ProductPackagingView product={item.product} size="xs" />
                </div>

                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 space-y-1">
                  <span className="text-[9px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] uppercase font-bold block">
                    {item.product.stepNumber}
                  </span>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-[#20231F] truncate font-serif-editorial">
                    {item.product.name}
                  </h4>
                  <div className="text-xs font-mono-spec font-semibold text-[#20231F]">
                    ₹{item.product.price}
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[2px] bg-[#F8F5EF]">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onUpdateQuantity(item.product.id, -1)}
                        className="px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-[#20231F] hover:bg-[#E8E1D6]"
                      >
                        <Minus className="w-3 h-3" />
                      </button>
                      <span className="px-2 text-[11px] font-mono-spec font-semibold text-[#20231F]">
                        {item.quantity}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => onUpdateQuantity(item.product.id, 1)}
                        className="px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-[#20231F] hover:bg-[#E8E1D6]"
                      >
                        <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => onRemoveItem(item.product.id)}
                      className="text-[#5C625B] hover:text-[#A65F5F] p-1"
                      title="Remove item"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="text-right font-mono-spec text-xs font-bold text-[#20231F]">
                  ₹{item.product.price * item.quantity}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Bottom Checkout Summary */}
        {items.length > 0 && (
          <div className="p-6 bg-[#F2EEE7] border-t border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-4 text-left">
            {/* Promo Code Box */}
            <form onSubmit={handleApplyPromo} className="space-y-1">
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Promo Code (try AEGIS10)"
                  value={promoCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setPromoCode(e.target.value)}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 bg-[#F8F5EF] border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[3px] text-xs font-mono-spec text-[#20231F] uppercase focus:outline-hidden"
                />
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-[#20231F] text-[#F8F5EF] text-xs font-mono-spec uppercase rounded-[3px]"
                >
                  Apply
                </button>
              </div>
              {promoError && (
                <p className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#A65F5F]">{promoError}</p>
              )}
              {appliedPromo && (
                <p className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848]">
                  ✓ Promo '{appliedPromo.code}' applied ({appliedPromo.discountPercent}% OFF)
                </p>
              )}
            </form>

            {/* Calculations */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] pt-2 border-t border-[#CFC8BC]/60">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span>SUBTOTAL</span>
                <span className="text-[#20231F]">₹{rawSubtotal}</span>
              </div>
              {appliedPromo && (
                <div className="flex justify-between text-[#4B5848]">
                  <span>DISCOUNT ({appliedPromo.code})</span>
                  <span>- ₹{discountAmount}</span>
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span>ESTIMATED DELIVERY</span>
                <span className="text-[#20231F]">
                  {shipping === 0 ? 'FREE' : `₹${shipping}`}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm font-bold text-[#20231F] pt-2 border-t border-[#CFC8BC]">
                <span>TOTAL</span>
                <span>₹{total}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Checkout Action */}
            <button
              id="cart-drawer-checkout-btn"
              onClick={() => {
                onClose();
                onOpenCheckout();
              }}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-[#4B5848] hover:bg-[#394536] text-[#F8F5EF] font-mono-spec text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-semibold rounded-[3px] transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-xs"
            >
              <span>PROCEED TO CHECKOUT · ₹{total}</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B]">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#4B5848]" />
              <span>100% Secure Encrypted Indian Checkout</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

==============================
FILE: src/components/HeroCanvas.tsx
==============================
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export const HeroCanvas: React.FC = () => {
  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    let animationFrameId: number;
    let width = (canvas.width = window.innerWidth);
    let height = (canvas.height = window.innerHeight);

    const handleResize = () => {
      if (!canvas) return;
      width = canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      height = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
    };
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);

    // Subtle, slow-moving architectural particles
    const particles: { x: number; y: number; vx: number; vy: number; radius: number }[] = [];
    const count = Math.min(30, Math.floor(width / 45));

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      particles.push({
        x: Math.random() * width,
        y: Math.random() * height,
        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.25,
        vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.25,
        radius: Math.random() * 1.2 + 0.8
      });
    }

    const render = () => {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      // Draw subtle warm mineral lines between close points
      for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
        const p = particles[i];
        p.x += p.vx;
        p.y += p.vy;

        if (p.x < 0) p.x = width;
        if (p.x > width) p.x = 0;
        if (p.y < 0) p.y = height;
        if (p.y > height) p.y = 0;

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(75, 88, 72, 0.25)'; // deep olive tone
        ctx.fill();

        for (let j = i + 1; j < particles.length; j++) {
          const p2 = particles[j];
          const dx = p.x - p2.x;
          const dy = p.y - p2.y;
          const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

          if (dist < 140) {
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y);
            ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);
            ctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(154, 128, 108, ${0.09 * (1 - dist / 140)})`; // muted clay line
            ctx.lineWidth = 0.6;
            ctx.stroke();
          }
        }
      }

      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);
    };

    render();

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);
      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);
    };
  }, []);

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none opacity-60" />;
};

==============================
FILE: src/components/ImageUploadButton.tsx
==============================
import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import { Camera, Link, Copy } from 'lucide-react';

interface Props {
  onImageUploaded: (dataUrl: string) => void;
  className?: string;
  variant?: 'icon' | 'full';
  currentImage?: string;
}

export const ImageUploadButton: React.FC<Props> = ({ onImageUploaded, className, variant = 'full', currentImage }) => {
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        const MAX_SIZE = 800; // Cap at 800px to avoid filling localStorage too fast
        let width = img.width;
        let height = img.height;

        if (width > height) {
          if (width > MAX_SIZE) {
            height *= MAX_SIZE / width;
            width = MAX_SIZE;
          }
        } else {
          if (height > MAX_SIZE) {
            width *= MAX_SIZE / height;
            height = MAX_SIZE;
          }
        }

        canvas.width = width;
        canvas.height = height;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (ctx) {
          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
          const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
          onImageUploaded(dataUrl);
        }
      };
      img.src = event.target?.result as string;
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    
    if (fileInputRef.current) {
      fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (currentImage) {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem('copied_image', currentImage);
        alert('Image copied to clipboard buffer!');
      } catch (err) {
        alert('Failed to copy image.');
      }
    }
  };

  
  const handlePaste = async (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    
    // Try to read from real clipboard if browser supports it
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.read) {
        const clipboardItems = await navigator.clipboard.read();
        for (const clipboardItem of clipboardItems) {
          const imageTypes = clipboardItem.types.filter(type => type.startsWith('image/'));
          for (const imageType of imageTypes) {
            const blob = await clipboardItem.getType(imageType);
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (event) => {
              const img = new Image();
              img.onload = () => {
                const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
                const MAX_SIZE = 800;
                let width = img.width;
                let height = img.height;
                if (width > height) {
                  if (width > MAX_SIZE) {
                    height *= MAX_SIZE / width;
                    width = MAX_SIZE;
                  }
                } else {
                  if (height > MAX_SIZE) {
                    width *= MAX_SIZE / height;
                    height = MAX_SIZE;
                  }
                }
                canvas.width = width;
                canvas.height = height;
                const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
                if (ctx) {
                  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);
                  const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8);
                  onImageUploaded(dataUrl);
                }
              };
              img.src = event.target?.result as string;
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
            return; // success!
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.log('Clipboard API failed or denied:', err);
    }

    const copied = localStorage.getItem('copied_image');
    if (copied) {
      onImageUploaded(copied);
    } else {
      const url = prompt("No image in buffer. Paste an image URL here instead (or use the upload button):");
      if (url) {
        onImageUploaded(url);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className={`flex gap-1 ${className || ''}`}>
      <button
        onClick={(e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          fileInputRef.current?.click();
        }}
        className={`flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md text-white rounded-[3px] transition-colors ${
          variant === 'icon' ? 'p-2' : 'gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono-spec'
        }`}
        title="Upload Custom Image"
      >
        <Camera className="w-4 h-4" />
        {variant === 'full' && <span>Upload</span>}
      </button>
      <button
        onClick={handleCopy}
        className={`flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md text-white rounded-[3px] transition-colors ${
          variant === 'icon' ? 'p-2' : 'gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono-spec'
        }`}
        title="Copy Image"
      >
        <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />
        {variant === 'full' && <span>Copy</span>}
      </button>
      <button
        onClick={handlePaste}
        className={`flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 hover:bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md text-white rounded-[3px] transition-colors ${
          variant === 'icon' ? 'p-2' : 'gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-mono-spec'
        }`}
        title="Paste Image URL"
      >
        <Link className="w-4 h-4" />
        {variant === 'full' && <span>Paste URL</span>}
      </button>
      <input
        type="file"
        ref={fileInputRef}
        onChange={handleFileChange}
        accept="image/*"
        className="hidden"
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Product } from "../types";
import { PRODUCTS, REVIEWS } from "../data/products";
import {
  X,
  Star,
  Check,
  Truck,
  RotateCcw,
  Heart,
  Plus,
  Minus,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldCheck,
  HelpCircle,
  MapPin,
  Sparkles,
  Info,
  Clock,
  Droplets,
  AlertTriangle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { AegisMonogram } from "./AegisMonogram";
import { ProductPackagingView } from "./ProductPackagingView";
import { useImageStore } from "../hooks/useImageStore";
import { ImageUploadButton } from "./ImageUploadButton";

interface ProductDetailModalProps {
  product: Product;
  onClose: () => void;
  onAddToCart: (product: Product, quantity?: number) => void;
  onAddMultipleToCart?: (products: Product[]) => void;
  onToggleWishlist: (productId: string) => void;
  onSelectProduct: (productId: string) => void;
  isWishlisted: boolean;
  onShowToast: (msg: string) => void;
}


const PairedProductCard: React.FC<{ pair: Product, onSelect: (id: string) => void }> = ({ pair, onSelect }) => {
  const { image } = useImageStore(pair.id, pair.image);
  return (
    <div
      onClick={() => onSelect(pair.id)}
      className="cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2 p-2 bg-[#F2EEE7] hover:bg-[#E8E1D6] rounded-[3px] border border-[#CFC8BC] transition-colors"
    >
      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-[2px] overflow-hidden shrink-0">
        <img
          src={image}
          alt={pair.name}
          className="w-full h-full object-cover"
          referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
        />
      </div>
      <div className="text-[11px] font-mono-spec">
        <span className="block text-[#20231F] font-semibold">
          {pair.name}
        </span>
        <span className="text-[#5C625B]">₹{pair.price}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export const ProductDetailModal: React.FC<ProductDetailModalProps> = ({
  product,
  onClose,
  onAddToCart,
  onAddMultipleToCart,
  onToggleWishlist,
  onSelectProduct,
  isWishlisted,
  onShowToast,
}) => {
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1);
  const { image, setCustomImage } = useImageStore(product.id, product.image);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<
    "why" | "ingredients" | "how" | "compatibility" | "inci" | "faq"
  >("why");
  const [pincode, setPincode] = useState("");
  const [deliveryStatus, setDeliveryStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [reviewFilterRating, setReviewFilterRating] = useState<number | null>(
    null,
  );

  const handleCheckPincode = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (/^\d{6}$/.test(pincode.trim())) {
      setDeliveryStatus(
        "✓ Express 2–4 day delivery available for PIN " +
          pincode +
          " · Free delivery on this order",
      );
    } else {
      setDeliveryStatus("Please enter a valid 6-digit Indian PIN code.");
    }
  };

  const pairedProducts = (product.pairsWith || [])
    .map((id) => PRODUCTS.find((p) => p.id === id))
    .filter(Boolean) as Product[];

  const completeRoutineProducts = (product.completeRoutineItemIds || [])
    .map((id) => PRODUCTS.find((p) => p.id === id))
    .filter(Boolean) as Product[];

  const routineTotalPrice = completeRoutineProducts.reduce(
    (sum, p) => sum + p.price,
    0,
  );
  const routineDiscount =
    product.completeRoutineDiscount || (routineTotalPrice > 1500 ? 348 : 0);
  const routineFinalPrice = routineTotalPrice - routineDiscount;

  const handleAddFullRoutine = () => {
    if (onAddMultipleToCart && completeRoutineProducts.length > 0) {
      onAddMultipleToCart(completeRoutineProducts);
      onShowToast(
        "Full 3-step routine added to bag with ₹" +
          routineDiscount +
          " savings.",
      );
      onClose();
    } else {
      onAddToCart(product);
      onClose();
    }
  };

  const filteredReviews = reviewFilterRating
    ? REVIEWS.filter((r) => r.rating === reviewFilterRating)
    : REVIEWS;

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-[#20231F]/70 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-2 sm:p-4 lg:p-6 animate-in fade-in"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="bg-[#F8F5EF] border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[4px] w-full max-w-5xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl relative my-6 text-left"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Close Button */}
        <button
          id="product-modal-close-btn"
          onClick={onClose}
          className="absolute top-4 right-4 z-20 p-2 rounded-full bg-[#F2EEE7] text-[#20231F] hover:bg-[#E8E1D6] transition-colors border border-[#CFC8BC]"
          aria-label="Close modal"
        >
          <X className="w-5 h-5" />
        </button>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-0">
          {/* Left Gallery & Quick Spec */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 bg-[#F2EEE7] p-6 sm:p-8 border-b lg:border-b-0 lg:border-r border-[#CFC8BC] flex flex-col justify-between space-y-6">
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">
                  {product.stepNumber}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] uppercase">
                  {product.category.toUpperCase()}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Main Visual Display Area */}
              <div className="aspect-square bg-[#151714] rounded-[3px] p-0 flex items-center justify-center border border-[#CFC8BC] relative overflow-hidden group/img">
                <div className="w-full h-full">
                  <img
                    src={image}
                    alt={product.name}
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover transition-transform duration-500 group-hover/img:scale-[1.02]"
                    referrerPolicy="no-referrer"
                  />
                  <div className="absolute top-2 left-2 z-30 opacity-0 group-hover/img:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                    <ImageUploadButton variant="icon" onImageUploaded={setCustomImage} currentImage={image} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <h2 className="font-serif-editorial text-3xl font-medium text-[#20231F] leading-tight">
                  {product.name}
                </h2>
                <p className="text-[#5C625B] text-sm">{product.subtitle}</p>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] pt-2">
                  <div className="flex text-[#4B5848]">
                    {Array.from({ length: Math.floor(product.rating) }).map(
                      (_, i) => (
                        <Star key={i} className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
                      ),
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-[#20231F]">
                    {product.rating}
                  </span>
                  <span>({product.reviewCount} reviews)</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Sticky Actions Toolbar */}
            <div className="p-4 bg-[#F2EEE7] border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[4px] flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex items-center border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[3px] bg-[#F8F5EF]">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setQuantity(Math.max(1, quantity - 1))}
                  className="px-3 py-2 text-[#20231F] hover:bg-[#E8E1D6] text-xs font-mono-spec"
                  aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                >
                  <Minus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
                <span className="px-3 text-xs font-mono-spec font-semibold text-[#20231F]">
                  {quantity}
                </span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setQuantity(quantity + 1)}
                  className="px-3 py-2 text-[#20231F] hover:bg-[#E8E1D6] text-xs font-mono-spec"
                  aria-label="Increase quantity"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <button
                id="modal-add-to-bag-btn"
                onClick={() => {
                  onAddToCart(product, quantity);
                  onClose();
                }}
                className="flex-1 py-3 px-6 rounded-[3px] bg-[#4B5848] hover:bg-[#394536] text-[#F8F5EF] font-mono-spec text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <span>Add to Bag · ₹{product.price * quantity}</span>
                <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>

              <button
                id="modal-wishlist-btn"
                onClick={() => onToggleWishlist(product.id)}
                className={`p-3 rounded-[3px] border transition-colors ${
                  isWishlisted
                    ? "border-[#A65F5F] text-[#A65F5F] bg-[#F8F5EF]"
                    : "border-[#CFC8BC] text-[#5C625B] hover:text-[#20231F] bg-[#F8F5EF]"
                }`}
                title="Save formulation"
              >
                <Heart
                  className={`w-4 h-4 ${isWishlisted ? "fill-current" : ""}`}
                />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Before You Buy Section */}
            {product.beforeYouBuy && (
              <div className="p-4 bg-[#F2EEE7] border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[4px] space-y-3">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block">
                  BEFORE YOU BUY
                </span>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-xs">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] uppercase block">
                      Finish
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-[#20231F]">
                      {product.beforeYouBuy.finish}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] uppercase block">
                      Fragrance
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-[#20231F]">
                      {product.beforeYouBuy.fragrance}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] uppercase block">
                      Best For
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-[#20231F]">
                      {product.beforeYouBuy.skinType}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] uppercase block">
                      Placement
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-[#20231F]">
                      {product.beforeYouBuy.routine}

                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] uppercase block">
                      Supply Duration
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-[#20231F]">
                      {product.beforeYouBuy.expectedUse}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* How to Use Timeline */}
            {product.howToUseTimeline && (
              <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block">

                </span>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  {product.howToUseTimeline.map((step, i) => (

                  placeholder="e.g. 560038 / 110001"
                  value={pincode}
                  onChange={(e) => setPincode(e.target.value)}
                  maxLength={6}
                  className="px-3 py-2 bg-[#F8F5EF] border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[3px] text-xs font-mono-spec text-[#20231F] w-44 focus:outline-hidden"
                />

                  type="submit"
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#20231F] hover:bg-[#343A33] text-[#F8F5EF] rounded-[3px] text-xs font-mono-spec uppercase font-semibold transition-colors"
                >
                  Verify

                  {deliveryStatus}

            </form>

            {/* Complete Your Routine (Bundled Section) */}
            {completeRoutineProducts.length > 1 && (

                      COMPLETE THE PROTOCOL

                      The 3-Step Daily System

                      ₹{routineTotalPrice}

                    <span className="text-base font-bold text-[#F8F5EF]">
                      ₹{routineFinalPrice}
                    </span>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                  {completeRoutineProducts.map((item) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.id}
                      className="p-2 bg-[#282C27] rounded-[3px] border border-[#3E453D] text-[10px] font-mono-spec space-y-1"
                    >
                      <span className="text-[#A9B7B7] block">
                        {item.stepCategory}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[#F8F5EF] font-semibold block truncate">
                        {item.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[#CFC8BC]">₹{item.price}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>

                <button
                  id="modal-add-routine-bundle-btn"
                  onClick={handleAddFullRoutine}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 bg-[#4B5848] hover:bg-[#394536] text-[#F8F5EF] rounded-[3px] text-xs font-mono-spec uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#A9B7B7]" />
                  <span>Add Full 3-Step System (Save ₹{routineDiscount})</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Detail Tabs */}
            <div className="space-y-4 pt-4 border-t border-[#CFC8BC]">
              <div className="flex border-b border-[#CFC8BC] text-xs font-mono-spec overflow-x-auto gap-4">
                {(
                  [
                    { id: "why", label: "Why It Exists" },
                    { id: "ingredients", label: "Key Actives" },
                    { id: "how", label: "How to Use" },
                    { id: "compatibility", label: "Compatibility" },
                    { id: "inci", label: "Full INCI" },
                    { id: "faq", label: "FAQ" },
                  ] as const
                ).map((tab) => (
                  <button
                    key={tab.id}
                    onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                    className={`pb-2 uppercase transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${
                      activeTab === tab.id
                        ? "border-b-2 border-[#4B5848] text-[#20231F] font-bold"
                        : "text-[#5C625B] hover:text-[#20231F]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {tab.label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Tab Content */}
              <div className="text-xs text-[#20231F] leading-relaxed space-y-3 min-h-[140px]">
                {activeTab === "why" && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <p className="text-[#5C625B] leading-relaxed">
                      {product.whyItExists}
                    </p>
                    <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-2">
                      <span className="font-mono-spec font-bold text-[11px] text-[#4B5848] uppercase block">
                        Observed Benefits:
                      </span>
                      {product.benefits.map((b, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                          <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#4B5848] mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                          <span>{b}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {activeTab === "ingredients" && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {product.keyActives.map((act, i) => (
                      <div
                        key={i}
                        className="p-3 bg-[#F2EEE7] rounded-[3px] border border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-1"
                      >
                        <div className="flex justify-between font-mono-spec text-[11px]">
                          <strong className="text-[#20231F]">
                            {act.name} ({act.concentration})
                          </strong>
                          <span className="text-[#4B5848]">{act.role}</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[#5C625B] text-[11px]">
                          {act.mechanism}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}

                {activeTab === "how" && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <div className="p-3 bg-[#F2EEE7] rounded-[3px] border border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase block">
                        Morning Protocol (AM):
                      </span>
                      <p className="text-[#5C625B]">{product.protocolAM}</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3 bg-[#F2EEE7] rounded-[3px] border border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-1">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase block">
                        Evening Protocol (PM):
                      </span>
                      <p className="text-[#5C625B]">{product.protocolPM}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {activeTab === "compatibility" && (
                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    {product.compatibility ? (
                      <div className="space-y-3">
                        <div className="p-3 bg-[#F2EEE7] rounded-[3px] border border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-1">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase block">
                            Works Well With:
                          </span>
                          <p className="text-[#20231F]">
                            {product.compatibility.worksWellWith.join(", ")}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="p-3 bg-[#F2EEE7] rounded-[3px] border border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-1">
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#A65F5F] font-bold uppercase block">
                            Use Carefully With:
                          </span>
                          <p className="text-[#5C625B]">
                            {product.compatibility.useCarefullyWith.join(", ")}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-[#5C625B] italic">
                          {product.compatibility.explanation}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-[#5C625B]">
                        Universally compatible with all AEGIS barrier care
                        formulations.
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>
                )}

                {activeTab === "inci" && (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] uppercase block">
                      100% Disclosed INCI List:
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-[11px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B] bg-[#F2EEE7] p-3 rounded-[3px] border border-[#CFC8BC] leading-relaxed">
                      {product.fullIngredients}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                )}

                {activeTab === "faq" && (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {product.faqList.map((faq, i) => (
                      <div
                        key={i}
                        className="space-y-1 border-b border-[#CFC8BC]/50 pb-2"
                      >
                        <strong className="text-[#20231F] block">
                          {faq.question}
                        </strong>
                        <p className="text-[#5C625B] text-[11px]">
                          {faq.answer}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Reviews Section with Star Filter */}
            <div className="pt-6 border-t border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest block">
                    COMMUNITY FEEDBACK
                  </span>
                  <h4 className="font-serif-editorial text-lg text-[#20231F]">
                    Customer Reviews ({product.reviewCount})
                  </h4>
                </div>

                <div className="flex gap-1 text-[10px] font-mono-spec">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setReviewFilterRating(null)}
                    className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-[2px] border ${
                      reviewFilterRating === null
                        ? "bg-[#20231F] text-[#F8F5EF] border-[#20231F]"
                        : "border-[#CFC8BC] text-[#5C625B]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    All
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setReviewFilterRating(5)}
                    className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-[2px] border ${
                      reviewFilterRating === 5
                        ? "bg-[#20231F] text-[#F8F5EF] border-[#20231F]"
                        : "border-[#CFC8BC] text-[#5C625B]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    5★
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setReviewFilterRating(4)}
                    className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-[2px] border ${
                      reviewFilterRating === 4
                        ? "bg-[#20231F] text-[#F8F5EF] border-[#20231F]"
                        : "border-[#CFC8BC] text-[#5C625B]"
                    }`}
                  >
                    4★
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {filteredReviews.slice(0, 3).map((r) => (
                  <div
                    key={r.id}
                    className="p-3 bg-[#F2EEE7] border border-[#CFC8BC] rounded-[3px] space-y-1.5"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-bold text-[#20231F]">
                          {r.author}
                        </span>
                        {r.verified && (
                          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono-spec bg-[#E8E1D6] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-[2px] text-[#4B5848]">
                            Verified Buyer
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#5C625B]">
                        {r.date}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex text-[#4B5848]">
                      {Array.from({ length: r.rating }).map((_, i) => (
                        <Star key={i} className="w-3 h-3 fill-current" />
                      ))}
                    </div>

                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#20231F]">
                      {r.title}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#5C625B] leading-relaxed">
                      {r.comment}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Pairs Well With */}
            {pairedProducts.length > 0 && (
              <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#CFC8BC] space-y-2">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono-spec text-[#4B5848] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider block">
                  PAIRS WELL WITH:
                </span>
                <div className="flex gap-3 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
                  {pairedProducts.map((pair) => (
                    <PairedProductCard key={pair.id} pair={pair} onSelect={onSelectProduct} />
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


==============================
FILE: src/components/Toast.tsx
==============================
import React from 'react';
import { Check, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface ToastProps {
  message: string | null;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const Toast: React.FC<ToastProps> = ({ message, onClose }) => {
  if (!message) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-5 duration-300">
      <div className="bg-[#20231F] text-[#F8F5EF] border border-[#3E453D] px-4 py-3 rounded-[4px] shadow-xl flex items-center gap-3 text-xs font-mono-spec">
        <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-[#4B5848] text-[#F8F5EF] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
          <Check className="w-3 h-3" />
        </div>
        <span className="font-medium">{message}</span>
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          className="text-[#A9B7B7] hover:text-[#F8F5EF] p-1 ml-2"
          aria-label="Dismiss notification"
        >
          <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export function calculateQuizResults(answers: Record<number, string>): QuizDiagnosis {
  const q1 = answers[1] || 'combo';
  const q2 = answers[2] || 'breakouts';
  const q3 = answers[3] || 'shave-trim';

  let skinType = 'Combination + Balanced';
  let barrierScore = 80;
  let oilScore = 65;
  let sensitivityScore = 35;
  let mainIssue = 'Occasional T-zone shine & everyday urban exposure';
  let priorityTitle = 'BALANCE & PROTECT';
  let priorityDescription = 'Maintain steady hydration while fortifying the acid mantle and shielding skin from daily daytime UV rays.';
  let targetActives = ['3:1:1 Ceramides', 'pH 5.5 Amino Acids', 'Broad-Spectrum SPF 50+ PA++++'];
  let recommendedProductIds = ['aegis-starter-bundle', 'aegis-wash', 'aegis-hydra', 'aegis-shield'];

  if (q1 === 'oily-all' || q2 === 'breakouts' || q2 === 'oil-control') {
    skinType = 'Oily + Congestion-Prone';
    barrierScore = 68;
    oilScore = 86;
    sensitivityScore = 42;
    mainIssue = 'Excess surface oil and clogged pores';
    priorityTitle = 'CONTROL CONGESTION';
    priorityDescription = 'Dissolve trapped sebum with lipophilic BHA and regulate surface oil appearance with 10% Niacinamide without stripping your moisture barrier.';
    targetActives = ['2.0% Salicylic Acid (BHA)', '10.0% Niacinamide', '1.0% Zinc PCA', 'Apple Amino Acids'];
    recommendedProductIds = ['aegis-clear-routine', 'aegis-clear', 'aegis-purify', 'aegis-matte'];
  } else if (q1 === 'tight-dry' || q2 === 'razor-burn' || q3 === 'shave-daily') {
    skinType = 'Dry + Barrier-Stressed';
    barrierScore = 58;
    oilScore = 32;
    sensitivityScore = 78;
    mainIssue = 'Post-shave sensitivity and low moisture retention';
    priorityTitle = 'RESTORE THE BARRIER';
    priorityDescription = 'Replenish lost intercellular lipids with biomimetic 3:1:1 Ceramides and soothe shaving friction with Centella and Ectoin.';
    targetActives = ['3:1:1 Biomimetic Ceramides', 'Polyglutamic Acid', 'Apple Amino Acids', '1.0% Ectoin'];
    recommendedProductIds = ['aegis-repair', 'aegis-recover', 'aegis-wash', 'aegis-shield'];
  } else if (q2 === 'daily-armor') {
    skinType = 'Everyday Urban + Photo-Defense';
    barrierScore = 84;
    oilScore = 55;
    sensitivityScore = 30;
    mainIssue = 'Daily sun exposure, blue light, and environmental protection';
    priorityTitle = 'DAILY DEFENSE';
    priorityDescription = 'Keep skin resilient with broad-spectrum photoprotection, antioxidant green tea, and barrier-conscious cleansing.';
    targetActives = ['Broad-Spectrum SPF 50+ PA++++', '1.0% Ectoin', 'Green Tea Extract', 'Matte Silica'];
    recommendedProductIds = ['aegis-starter-bundle', 'aegis-shield', 'aegis-clear', 'aegis-wash'];
  }

  return {
    skinType,
    barrierScore,
    oilScore,
    sensitivityScore,
    mainIssue,
    priorityTitle,
    priorityDescription,
    targetActives,
    recommendedProductIds,
    amSteps: [
      {
        step: '01. CLEANSE (30s)',
        product: 'AEGIS WASH',
        instruction: 'Lather 1 pump on damp skin to remove overnight oils without stripping moisture.'
      },
      {
        step: '02. CORRECT (30s)',
        product: 'AEGIS CLEAR',
        instruction: 'Apply 2-3 drops to help balance oil and keep pore openings clear.'
      },
      {
        step: '03. DEFEND (60s)',
        product: 'AEGIS SHIELD SPF 50',
        instruction: 'Apply 2 finger lengths evenly. Absorbs 100% clear with a clean matte finish.'
      }
    ],
    pmSteps: [
      {
        step: '01. CLEANSE (30s)',
        product: 'AEGIS WASH',
        instruction: 'Wash away daytime pollution, sweat, and sunscreen.'
      },
      {
        step: '02. TREAT / REPAIR (30s)',
        product: 'AEGIS CLEAR or BARRIER',
        instruction: 'Apply serum for pore clarity, or barrier fluid for post-shave comfort.'
      }
    ],
    guidanceNote: 'This quiz provides general skincare guidance and is not a medical diagnosis. For chronic dermatological conditions, consult a medical professional.'
  };
}

      '## 1. Quantitative Dermal Thickness & Collagen Kinetics',
      'In dermatological literature, human sexual dimorphism in skin architecture is often oversimplified as mere "oiliness." However, high-resolution ultrasound biomicroscopy and punch-biopsy histopathology reveal structural divergences that govern cellular turnover, structural integrity, and barrier vulnerability.',
      'On average, the male dermis is between 20% and 25% thicker than that of age-matched females. This increased caliper is primarily attributed to a denser, more tightly woven matrix of Type I and Type III collagen bundles in the reticular dermis, supported by an enriched proteoglycan gel ground substance. Androgenic stimulation directly upregulates procollagen mRNA transcription in dermal fibroblasts during post-pubertal development.',
      'Crucially, whereas female collagen density experiences an accelerated decline post-menopause due to precipitous estrogen depletion, male dermal collagen declines at a continuous, steady linear rate of approximately 1% per year beginning in the mid-twenties. This means male skin preserves mechanical tensile strength longer, but because environmental photo-exposure is historically under-addressed by men, the cumulative UV insult ultimately produces deeper, more pronounced elastotic furrows.',
      '## 2. Androgens & Sebaceous Gland Pathophysiology',
      'The primary hormonal driver of male cutaneous biology is dihydrotestosterone (DHT), converted locally in the sebaceous gland by the enzyme 5-alpha reductase (Type I isoform). The density of androgen receptors within male facial sebaceous glands is markedly higher in the mandibular, perioral, and mid-facial zones.',
      'This androgenic signaling commands sebocytes to synthesize and secrete elevated volumes of sebum. Under physiological conditions, sebum functions as an indispensable protective mantle: a lipidic emulsion composed of triglycerides, wax esters, squalene, and free fatty acids that lubricates the hair follicle and delivers fat-soluble antioxidants (notably alpha-tocopherol) to the stratum corneum.',
      '> "The clinical paradox of male skin: an abundant lipid output on the surface frequently masks a profoundly dehydrated, compromised intercellular moisture barrier beneath."',
      'When sebum output is chronically elevated, two pathological cascades emerge. First, squalene—a unique unsaturated hydrocarbon constituting roughly 12-15% of sebum—is exceptionally prone to photo-oxidation when exposed to solar UVA radiation and ambient ozone. Oxidized squalene (squalene monohydroperoxide) triggers inflammatory cytokine cascades (IL-1alpha, IL-6, and TNF-alpha), promoting comedogenesis and peripilary erythema.',
      'Second, the high rate of lipid flux frequently leads men to reach for aggressive, high-pH alkaline foaming detergents. These products dissolve the superficial lipid film but simultaneously extract vital structural ceramides from the stratum corneum, creating dehydrated, tight skin with paradoxical rebound hyper-seborrhea.',
      '## 3. The Razor Blade as a Daily Physical Exfoliant',
      'Perhaps the most clinically significant environmental insult unique to male facial skin is mechanical shaving. Over a lifetime of daily grooming, the average man subjects his lower facial dermis to approximately 20,000 passes of high-tensile surgical steel.',
      'While often categorized as an aesthetic routine, shaving is biologically an involuntary, aggressive micro-dermabrasion. A sharp razor blade does not merely sever the hair shaft at the follicular orifice; it strips away two to three superficial layers of anucleated corneocytes along with their surrounding multi-lamellar lipid bilayers.',
      'Furthermore, the multi-blade cartridge systems prevalent in consumer grooming utilize a "lift and cut" mechanism (the hysteresis effect): the leading blade pulls the hair shaft upwards while subsequent blades sever it below the skin surface level. As the cut hair retracts back into the follicular sheath, its sharp, beveled edge can pierce the follicular wall or curl back into adjacent peripilary tissue, creating the inflammatory condition known as Pseudofolliculitis Barbae.',
      '## 4. Barrier Disruption & Pseudofolliculitis Mechanics',
      'The acute mechanical shear stress of razor passes triggers an immediate cutaneous response: trans-epidermal water loss (TEWL) in the shaved mandibular zone spikes by up to 35-50% immediately post-shave compared to non-shaved forehead regions. Cellular junctional integrity is compromised, permitting environmental particulate matter and topical irritants direct access to the viable nucleated epidermis.',
      'Traditionally, consumer aftershaves relied on high-proof denatured alcohol solutions (40-60% ethanol) designed to act as an antiseptic against Cutibacterium acnes. From a modern biochemical standpoint, this practice is disastrous: ethanol denatures native epidermal proteins, dissolves remaining barrier lipids, and sparks neurogenic stinging via TRPV1 heat/pain nociceptor activation.',
      'Instead, post-shave dermal recovery requires rapid physiological pH restoration (pH 5.0 to 5.5), barrier lipid restoration via biomimetic ceramides, and targeted anti-inflammatory signaling molecules such as Ectoin, Panthenol, and Centella Asiatica triterpenes to extinguish the inflammatory cytokine cascade before erythema solidifies.',
      '## 5. Evidence-Based Formulation Requirements',
      'Recognizing these unique biological markers dictates distinct clinical formulation criteria for male topical skincare:',
      '• **Surfactant Selection**: Strict elimination of sodium lauryl sulfate (SLS) and alkaline saponified soaps. Cleansers must utilize amino-acid based surfactants (such as sodium cocoyl glycinate or apple amino acid acylates) buffered precisely at pH 5.5 to preserve the acid mantle.',
      '• **Vehicle Viscosity & Penetration**: Because male facial epidermis has higher follicular density and sebum presence, heavy, occlusive petroleum-rich cold creams fail to absorb, remaining atop facial hair stubble as a greasy film. Formulations must utilize micro-emulsions, lamellar gel-creams, and volatile hemisqualane carriers that penetrate rapidly around terminal hair shafts without residue.',
      '• **Photo-Protection Compatibility**: Men consistently reject sunscreens that leave a white titanium/zinc cast trapped in beard stubble. Modern photoprotection must employ transparent organic hybrid filters (such as Tinosorb S, Uvinul A Plus) in water-burst, weightless vehicles that offer broad-spectrum SPF 50+ without follicular plugging.'

      'The stratum corneum is not dead tissue; it is a metabolically active biosensor maintaining enzymatic homeostasis and electrolyte gradients.',

    content: [
      '## 1. The Two-Compartment Model: Corneocytes & Intercellular Lipids',
      'For the first half of the twentieth century, classical physiology viewed the stratum corneum—the outermost 10 to 20 micrometers of human skin—as an inert sheath of biological "dead cells" whose only purpose was passive mechanical resistance. This outdated paradigm was definitively dismantled by Dr. Peter M. Elias in his seminal work establishing the "Brick and Mortar" structural model.',
      'In this functional architecture, the "bricks" are corneocytes: terminally differentiated, polygonal keratinocytes that have lost their nuclei and cytoplasmic organelles. The interior of each corneocyte is densely packed with organized macrofibrillar bundles of keratin encased within a cross-linked insoluble protein shell termed the cornified cell envelope (composed of loricrin, involucrin, and small proline-rich proteins).',
      'Bound covalently to the outer face of this envelope is the cornified lipid envelope: a single monomolecular layer of omega-hydroxyceramides that serves as an essential hydrophilic-lipophilic template. Between these corneocyte bricks lies the "mortar": continuous, highly ordered multi-lamellar lipid sheets. It is this intercellular lipid matrix—and strictly this matrix—that governs whether water remains locked inside the living viable epidermis or evaporates into the atmosphere.',
      '## 2. The 3:1:1 Equimolar Ratio & Lamellar Phases',
      'Unlike the phospholipid-rich fluid membranes of viable internal human cells, the intercellular lipids of the stratum corneum contain virtually zero phospholipids. Through a tightly coordinated differentiation program inside epidermal lamellar bodies (Odland bodies), phospholipids are enzymatically converted into a specialized hydrophobic cocktail:',
      '• **Ceramides (~50% by weight)**: Complex sphingolipids consisting of a sphingoid base linked to a fatty acid. Subtypes including Ceramide NP, Ceramide AP, and Ceramide EOP possess varying acyl chain lengths (up to 30-34 carbon atoms) capable of bridging adjacent lamellae.',
      '• **Cholesterol (~25% by weight)**: A rigid planar sterol molecule that inserts between flexible ceramide chains, stabilizing membrane fluidity across a broad temperature range.',
      '• **Free Fatty Acids (~15% by weight)**: Predominantly long-chain, straight, saturated fatty acids such as stearic (C18:0), palmitic (C16:0), and lignoceric (C24:0) acids.',
      '> "Seminal studies at the University of California have repeatedly demonstrated that applying any single lipid component in isolation (e.g., pure squalane or pure ceramides alone) can actually delay endogenous barrier recovery. True accelerated repair demands an equimolar 3:1:1 or 1:1:1 physiological lipid ratio."',
      'These three lipids self-assemble into two coexisting lamellar phases: a Long Periodicity Phase (LPP, ~13 nm repeat distance) and a Short Periodicity Phase (SPP, ~6 nm repeat distance). In healthy human skin, the hydrocarbon tails pack tightly into an "orthorhombic" crystalline gel lattice. This dense, crystalline arrangement presents a virtually impassable tortuous pathway for polar water molecules, restricting Transepidermal Water Loss (TEWL) to baseline healthy values (<10 g/m²/h).',
      '## 3. Enzymatic Regulation & The pH 5.5 Acid Mantle',
      'The assembly of this protective lipid mortar is not passive; it is strictly regulated by pH-dependent hydrolytic enzymes operating in the extracellular matrix. Healthy human stratum corneum maintains an acidic surface pH between 4.7 and 5.5—the historical "Acid Mantle."',
      'Two paramount barrier-building enzymes—**beta-glucocerebrosidase** (which converts glucosylceramides into functional ceramides) and **acid sphingomyelinase**—possess narrow acidic pH optima around 4.5 to 5.0. When skin pH is preserved within this range, ceramide production proceeds efficiently.',
      'Conversely, when the skin surface is shifted toward neutral or alkaline pH, a dangerous molecular switch occurs. Beta-glucocerebrosidase activity falls precipitously. Simultaneously, neutral serine proteases (such as kallikrein-related peptidases KLK5 and KLK7) become hyperactivated. These enzymes prematurely cleave the corneodesmosome protein rivets anchoring corneocytes together, triggering premature micro-flaking, visible redness, and desquamation failure.',
      '## 4. Surfactant-Induced Lipid Dissolution',
      'The primary cause of chronic barrier degradation in men is the daily use of alkaline bar soaps and high-active anionic surfactants (like sodium lauryl sulfate and saponified tallowates, with pH ranges between 9.0 and 10.5).',
      'When an alkaline soap touches the skin, three simultaneous insults unfold:',
      '1. **Lipid Extraction**: The surfactant micelles do not merely emulsify surface grime and sebum; they insert into the intercellular lamellae, solubilizing and extracting native cholesterol and ceramides into the rinse water.',
      '2. **Keratin Swelling**: The alkaline pH drives electrostatic repulsion between adjacent keratin filaments inside corneocytes, causing them to swell, absorb excess water during cleansing, and then hyper-contract and dry out upon evaporation (the classic "post-wash tight face" sensation).',
      '3. **Prolonged Alkaline Shift**: Normal skin buffering capacity requires between 4 and 8 hours to re-acidify following a single alkaline soap wash. If a man washes his face morning and evening with bar soap, his skin spends up to 16 hours every day in an enzyme-compromised alkaline state.',
      '## 5. Therapeutic Principles of Stratum Corneum Repair',
      'Reversing chronic barrier failure requires a targeted dermatological protocol focused on three clinical pillars:',
      '• **Phase 1: Physiological Cleansing (pH 5.0–5.5)**: Discontinue all saponified bars. Adopt non-ionic, amphoteric, or amino-acid surfactants that demonstrate minimal protein-binding affinity (Zein score < 15 mg N/100mL).',
      '• **Phase 2: Lamellar Replenishment (Multi-Lamellar Lipids)**: Apply biomimetic emulsions formulated with Ceramide NP, Ceramide AP, Ceramide EOP, Phytosphingosine, and plant-derived cholesterol in physiological ratios. These formulations integrate directly into damaged intercellular spaces, restoring the orthorhombic packing lattice within 48 to 72 hours.',
      '• **Phase 3: Osmolyte & Humectant Support**: Supplement with multi-molecular weight Hyaluronic Acid, Glycerin, and Ectoin. Low-molecular weight fractions penetrate into the upper spinosum to stimulate filaggrin expression, while high-molecular weight humectants create an invisible breathable moisture reservoir atop the stratum corneum.'

      '## 1. The Solar Spectrum: UVB, UVA-I, UVA-II, and HEV Light',
      'The optical radiation reaching the Earth\'s surface encompasses a continuous electromagnetic spectrum from ultraviolet (290–400 nm) through visible light (400–700 nm) to infrared radiation (700–1000+ nm). In popular perception, sun exposure is evaluated purely through erythema—the visible sunburn response. This cognitive bias is scientifically hazardous.',
      'Sunburn is overwhelmingly mediated by **UVB radiation (290–320 nm)**. UVB photons carry high quantal energy, which is directly absorbed by cellular DNA in the basal epidermis, producing signature cytosine-to-thymine transition mutations. However, UVB constitutes less than 5% of terrestrial ultraviolet radiation and is largely attenuated by commercial window glass and overcast cloud cover.',
      'In contrast, **UVA radiation (320–400 nm)** accounts for more than 95% of terrestrial UV. It is subdivided into UVA-II (320–340 nm) and UVA-I (340–400 nm). Crucially, the atmosphere does not filter UVA efficiently: UVA photons penetrate unbroken through thick cloud cover, automobile side windows, and architectural glazing 365 days a year, at virtually identical intensities at 9:00 AM as at 2:00 PM.',
      'Furthermore, UVA penetrates through the epidermis down into the reticular dermis, directly irradiating the vascular endothelium, dermal fibroblasts, and pericytes without producing the immediate burning sensation that prompts people to seek shade.',
      '## 2. Molecular Pathology: CPDs, ROS, and MMP-Mediated Elastosis',
      'The biological aftermath of UVA irradiation unfolds primarily through indirect photochemical mechanisms known as Type I and Type II photosensitized oxidation. Chromophores within human cells (such as riboflavin, porphyrins, and urocanic acid) absorb UVA photons and transfer excitation energy to ground-state molecular oxygen, generating **Reactive Oxygen Species (ROS)**—predominantly singlet oxygen (¹O₂), superoxide anion radicals (O₂•⁻), and hydrogen peroxide (H₂O₂).',
      'This deluge of oxidative stress triggers a destructive molecular cascade:',
      '• **Dark CPD Formation**: Research published in *Science* demonstrated that cyclobutane pyrimidine dimers (CPDs)—the primary DNA lesions responsible for skin cancer—continue to be synthesized in melanocytes for more than three hours *after* UV exposure has ceased, driven by chemiexcitation of melanin breakdown fragments.',
      '• **MMP Gene Induction**: Oxidative stress activates cell-surface cytokine receptors (IL-1, TNF-alpha) which stimulate the AP-1 and NF-kB transcription factors. This commands dermal fibroblasts to synthesize and release **Matrix Metalloproteinases (MMP-1 interstitial collagenase, MMP-3 stromelysin, and MMP-9 gelatinase)**.',
      '> "A single unprotected exposure to midday sunlight elevates dermal collagenase levels within hours. These enzymes actively digest collagen triple-helices and fragmented elastin for up to 48 to 72 hours thereafter, converting resilient dermis into amorphous, elastotic scar tissue."',
      '• **Telomere Shortening & Cellular Senescence**: Chronic UV irradiation accelerates telomeric DNA erosion in proliferating keratinocytes, forcing cells into permanent replicative senescence. Senescent cells secrete the Senescence-Associated Secretory Phenotype (SASP)—a toxic cocktail of pro-inflammatory cytokines that degrades neighboring healthy tissue.',
      '## 3. The Facial Stubble & Beard Fallacy',
      'A dangerous misconception pervasive among men is that maintaining facial stubble, a five o\'clock shadow, or a trimmed beard eliminates the need for sunscreen over the lower third of the face.',
      'In controlled spectrophotometric and ultraviolet transmission studies, the Ultraviolet Protection Factor (UPF) of a standard 2–5mm groomed beard ranges between a negligible **UPF 1.5 and UPF 3.0**. By comparison, the American Academy of Dermatology and World Health Organization establish a minimum protective threshold of UPF 30–50.',
      'Because hair density is non-uniform and hairs grow obliquely, UVA photons bounce between individual cuticle shafts, easily reaching the underlying inter-follicular skin. Consequently, dermatological oncology clinics routinely diagnose actinic keratoses and basal cell carcinomas in the beard beds of men who mistakenly assumed their hair provided impenetrable shade.',
      '## 4. Filter Chemistry: Mineral Dispersions vs Modern Organic Hybrids',
      'To defend skin against this full-spectrum bombardment, sunscreen formulations utilize UV filter compounds classified into physical (inorganic) and chemical (organic) categories.',
      '• **Inorganic Filters (Zinc Oxide, Titanium Dioxide)**: Mineral filters attenuate UV primarily by semiconductor band-gap absorption (95%) and physical scattering/reflection (5%). While inherently non-irritating and chemically inert, physical filters pose immense compliance challenges for men. To achieve broad-spectrum UVA-I attenuation with zinc oxide, high mass fractions (15-20%) are required. When suspended in a lotion, these micro-particles scatter visible light, creating a distinct chalky white cast that clings tenaciously to dark hair stubble and facial hair.',
      '• **Legacy Organic Filters (Oxybenzone, Octinoxate, Avobenzone)**: First-generation organic filters were widely criticized for photolability (avobenzone degrades up to 50% within one hour of direct sunlight unless heavily stabilized) and skin irritation in active, sweating men.',
      '• **Next-Generation Organic Hybrids (Tinosorb S, Uvinul A Plus, Tinosorb M)**: Approved throughout Europe, Australia, and Asia, modern large-molecular-weight hybrid filters represent a quantum leap in photobiology. Compounds like Bis-Ethylhexyloxyphenol Methoxyphenyl Triazine (Tinosorb S) possess molecular weights exceeding 500 Daltons—meaning they are too large to penetrate through the stratum corneum into systemic circulation. They exhibit near-perfect photostability (>98% retention after 2 hours), broad-spectrum coverage spanning UVB through UVA-I (290–380 nm), and total optical transparency.',
      '## 5. Overcoming Male Compliance Failures with Formulation Elegance',
      'The greatest sunscreen formulation in the world possesses zero clinical efficacy if the patient refuses to apply it every morning. Behavioral surveys reveal that 84% of men who do not wear daily sunscreen cite sensory objections: "greasiness," "eye stinging during workouts," "shiny forehead," and "white marks in beard hair."',
      'Eliminating these friction points requires specific engineering:',
      '1. **Silica-Silylate Sebum Absorbers**: Integrating porous spherical silica beads that selectively capture excess lipid droplets on the skin surface throughout the day, preventing mid-day glare.',
      '2. **Water-Gel Inversion Technology**: Formulating oil-in-water nano-emulsions that break immediately upon dermal friction, releasing a cool, aqueous surge that evaporates within 20 seconds, leaving a completely undetectable natural matte finish.',
      '3. **Biochemical Cellular Stress Protectors (Ectoin)**: Fortifying the formulation with 1.0% Ectoin—a natural extremolyte cyclic amino acid derivative. Ectoin forms tight, highly structured hydration shells around cellular membranes and proteins, shielding cell structures against UV-induced protein carbonylation and heat shock even during intense outdoor physical exertion.'

      '## 1. The Four Interconnected Pillars of Acne Genesis',
      'Acne vulgaris is frequently mischaracterized as an adolescent cosmetic inconvenience that resolves spontaneously with maturity. In clinical reality, adult male acne—manifesting as stubborn perioral papules, mandibular jawline cysts, and comedones across the T-zone—is a chronic inflammatory disorder of the pilosebaceous unit with distinct biomolecular triggers.',
      'The pathogenesis of adult follicular occlusion rests on four interconnected biological abnormalities:',
      '1. **Androgen-Driven Seborrhea**: Systemic testosterone and locally produced dihydrotestosterone (DHT) stimulate the enlargement of sebocytes, maximizing sebum flow rate.',
      '2. **Follicular Hyperkeratinization**: Keratinocytes lining the upper infundibular channel of the follicle fail to detach cleanly through normal desquamation. Instead, they adhere together in dense cohesive clumps, forming a microscopic plug (the microcomedone).',
      '3. **Microbiome Imbalance (Cutibacterium acnes Dysbiosis)**: The anaerobic environment within the plugged follicle permits selective proliferation of virulent C. acnes phylotypes.',
      '4. **Innate Immune Activation**: Release of chemokines, neutrophil infiltration, and rupture of the follicular epithelium into the surrounding dermis, generating visible erythematous nodules and inflammatory papules.',
      '## 2. Squalene Peroxidation & Inflammatory Mediators',
      'A pivotal discovery in dermatological lipidomics over the past decade is that the *quality and chemical composition* of sebum is just as decisive as the *quantity* of sebum produced.',
      'Normal human sebum contains approximately 12% squalene, a branched triterpenic hydrocarbon with six non-conjugated double bonds. Because of these unsaturated bonds, squalene acts as a preferential target for environmental oxidative stress (solar UVA radiation, cigarette smoke, and atmospheric ozone).',
      '> "When squalene undergoes photo-peroxidation, it transforms into squalene monohydroperoxide (SQOOH). SQOOH is biologically aggressive: it directly upregulates pro-inflammatory cytokines, stimulates hyperkeratinization within the follicular canal, and triggers toll-like receptor 2 (TLR-2) activation on surrounding dendritic cells."',
      'This explains why men who work in outdoor or urban industrial environments frequently suffer sudden breakouts despite rigorous washing: environmental oxidation of their own surface lipids is actively creating comedogenic compounds directly atop their skin.',
      '## 3. Cutibacterium acnes: Commensal vs Pathogenic Phylotypes',
      'The traditional assumption that all *Cutibacterium acnes* bacteria are inherently bad is medically obsolete. Metagenomic sequencing has revealed that *C. acnes* comprises multiple distinct genetic lineages (phylotypes: IA1, IA2, IB, IC, II, and III).',
      'Certain phylotypes (such as clade II and ribotype 6) are protective commensals that produce bacteriocins to repel invading pathogens and synthesize short-chain fatty acids (propionic acid) that maintain the healthy acidic pH of the skin mantle.',
      'In contrast, individuals suffering from inflammatory acne exhibit an over-representation of phylotype IA1 (ribotype 4 and 5). These virulent strains express elevated levels of Christie-Atkins-Munch-Petersen (CAMP) factor, hyaluronate lyase, and lipases that cleave neutral sebum triglycerides into irritating free fatty acids, inciting severe local tissue damage.',
      'Consequently, the clinical goal of modern acne management is never total sterilization—which triggers antimicrobial resistance and severe barrier dermatitis—but rather modulating the follicular microenvironment so commensal strains thrive while virulent overgrowth is curtailed.',
      '## 4. Exfoliation Mechanics: Salicylic Acid (BHA) vs Alpha Hydroxy Acids (AHA)',
      'When addressing follicular congestion, the chemical structure of the exfoliating agent determines its anatomical site of action.',
      '• **Alpha Hydroxy Acids (Glycolic Acid, Lactic Acid, Mandelic Acid)**: AHAs are water-soluble carboxylic acids with a hydroxyl group on the alpha carbon. Because they are strictly hydrophilic, AHAs cannot penetrate through the hydrophobic sebum plugs filling a pore. They operate exclusively on the surface stratum corneum by chelating calcium ions from corneodesmosomes, smoothing rough surface texture and accelerating epidermal renewal. While excellent for post-inflammatory hyperpigmentation (PIH), AHAs cannot clear clogged pores internally.',
      '• **Beta Hydroxy Acid (Salicylic Acid)**: Salicylic acid is an ortho-hydroxybenzoic acid derived from salicin. The presence of the aromatic benzene ring renders the molecule strongly **lipophilic (oil-soluble)**. Upon application, salicylic acid effortlessly dissolves into and traverses the sebum-filled infundibulum, penetrating deep inside the pore to sever desmosomal bonds between impacted follicular corneocytes.',
      'Furthermore, because salicylic acid is structurally related to acetylsalicylic acid (aspirin), it retains intrinsic anti-inflammatory properties, downregulating COX-2 prostaglandin synthesis and subduing redness at the very site of follicular irritation.',
      '## 5. The Dual Regulatory Power of Niacinamide (Vitamin B3)',
      'While Salicylic Acid physically clears existing impactions, **Niacinamide (Nicotinamide)** addresses the underlying biochemical defect.',
      'Niacinamide is the physiological precursor to nicotinamide adenine dinucleotide (NAD+/NADH) and its phosphorylated form (NADP+/NADPH)—the fundamental coenzymes powering cellular redox metabolism in human cells.',
      'In clinical dermatology trials, topical application of 2% to 5% Niacinamide yields two profound, distinct benefits:',
      '1. **Sebum Lipogenesis Downregulation**: Niacinamide inhibits the activity of key lipogenic enzymes inside sebocytes, producing a statistically significant reduction in casual sebum excretion rate (SER) within 2 to 4 weeks of consistent use.',
      '2. **Endogenous Ceramide Synthesis**: In direct contrast to harsh retinoids and drying benzoyl peroxide washes that strip the barrier, Niacinamide stimulates the synthesis of glucosylceramides and sphingomyelin in human keratinocytes, upregulating native barrier lipids by up to 50%.',
      'By pairing targeted lipophilic Salicylic Acid with restorative Niacinamide, an evidence-based formulation clears current impactions while simultaneously rebuilding barrier resilience against future dysbiosis.'

While the master circadian pacemaker resides in the suprachiasmatic nucleus (SCN) of the hypothalamus, landmark discoveries in chronobiology have revealed that virtually every peripheral organ—including human skin—possesses its own autonomous molecular clocks.',

      '• **pH Neutralization and Inactivation**: Certain gold-standard dermatological actives require strict acidic pH levels to function. FILE: src/services/chatService.ts
